---
name: frontend-design
description: Create distinctive, production-grade frontend interfaces with high design quality. Use this skill when the user asks to build web components, pages, artifacts, posters, or applications (examples include websites, landing pages, dashboards, React components, HTML/CSS layouts, or when styling/beautifying any web UI). Generates creative, polished code and UI design that avoids generic AI aesthetics.
---

This skill guides creation of distinctive, production-grade frontend interfaces that avoid generic "AI slop" aesthetics. Implement real working code with exceptional attention to aesthetic details and creative choices.

The user provides frontend requirements: a component, page, application, or interface to build. They may include context about the purpose, audience, or technical constraints.

## Design Thinking

Before coding, understand the context and commit to a BOLD aesthetic direction:
- **Purpose**: What problem does this interface solve? Who uses it?
- **Tone**: Pick an extreme: brutally minimal, maximalist chaos, retro-futuristic, organic/natural, luxury/refined, playful/toy-like, editorial/magazine, brutalist/raw, art deco/geometric, soft/pastel, industrial/utilitarian, etc. There are so many flavors to choose from. Use these for inspiration but design one that is true to the aesthetic direction.
- **Constraints**: Technical requirements (framework, performance, accessibility).
- **Differentiation**: What makes this UNFORGETTABLE? What's the one thing someone will remember?

**CRITICAL**: Choose a clear conceptual direction and execute it with precision. Bold maximalism and refined minimalism both work - the key is intentionality, not intensity.

Then implement working code (HTML/CSS/JS, React, Vue, etc.) that is:
- Production-grade and functional
- Visually striking and memorable
- Cohesive with a clear aesthetic point-of-view
- Meticulously refined in every detail

## Frontend Aesthetics Guidelines

Focus on:
- **Typography**: Choose fonts that are beautiful, unique, and interesting. Avoid generic fonts like Arial and Inter; opt instead for distinctive choices that elevate the frontend's aesthetics; unexpected, characterful font choices. Pair a distinctive display font with a refined body font.
- **Color & Theme**: Commit to a cohesive aesthetic. Use CSS variables for consistency. Dominant colors with sharp accents outperform timid, evenly-distributed palettes.
- **Motion**: Use animations for effects and micro-interactions. Prioritize CSS-only solutions for HTML. Use Motion library for React when available. Focus on high-impact moments: one well-orchestrated page load with staggered reveals (animation-delay) creates more delight than scattered micro-interactions. Use scroll-triggering and hover states that surprise.
- **Spatial Composition**: Unexpected layouts. Asymmetry. Overlap. Diagonal flow. Grid-breaking elements. Generous negative space OR controlled density.
- **Backgrounds & Visual Details**: Create atmosphere and depth rather than defaulting to solid colors. Add contextual effects and textures that match the overall aesthetic. Apply creative forms like gradient meshes, noise textures, geometric patterns, layered transparencies, dramatic shadows, decorative borders, custom cursors, and grain overlays.

NEVER use generic AI-generated aesthetics like overused font families (Inter, Roboto, Arial, system fonts), cliched color schemes (particularly purple gradients on white backgrounds), predictable layouts and component patterns, and cookie-cutter design that lacks context-specific character.

Interpret creatively and make unexpected choices that feel genuinely designed for the context. No design should be the same. Vary between light and dark themes, different fonts, different aesthetics. NEVER converge on common choices (Space Grotesk, for example) across generations.

**IMPORTANT**: Match implementation complexity to the aesthetic vision. Maximalist designs need elaborate code with extensive animations and effects. Minimalist or refined designs need restraint, precision, and careful attention to spacing, typography, and subtle details. Elegance comes from executing the vision well.

Remember: Claude is capable of extraordinary creative work. Don't hold back, show what can truly be created when thinking outside the box and committing fully to a distinctive vision.

 export function translate(locale: Locale, text: string): string {
@@ -3591,6 +4231,36 @@ export function translate(locale: Locale, text: string): string {

+// "{done} of {total}" -> "3 of 5". Values are shown as given.
+export function fill(
+  text: string,
+  values: Record<string, string | number>
+): string {
+  return text.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (m, k) =>
+    k in values ? String(values[k]) : m
+  );
+}
+
+// Count-dependent sentence. English picks `one` for 1, else `other`. Latvian
+// and Russian look up `${other}|${category}` (CLDR via Intl.PluralRules; Latvian
+// "zero" uses the "|other" entry), so "21 dienu" / "11 dienām" come out right.
+export function plural(
+  locale: Locale,
+  n: number,
+  one: string,
+  other: string
+): string {
+  let text = n === 1 ? one : other;
+  if (locale !== "en") {
+    const category = new Intl.PluralRules(locale).select(n);
+    const forms = [`${other}|${category}`, `${other}|other`];
+    text = translate(locale, forms[0]);
+    if (text === forms[0]) text = translate(locale, forms[1]);
+    if (text === forms[1]) text = translate(locale, other);
+  }
+  return fill(text, { n: formatNumber(n, locale) });
+}
+
 // Shared by every page rendering an Astro Action's error: never shows raw
 // Zod/ActionInputError JSON. When the action failed Zod input validation
 // (`error.fields` present), shows the first field's plain-language message,
@@ -3645,11 +4315,14 @@ export function formatNumber(
 export function formatMoney(
   value: string,
   currency: string,
-  locale: Locale
+  locale: Locale,
+  forceGrouping = false
 ): string {
-  return new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, { style: "currency", currency }).format(
-    Number(value)
-  );
+  return new Intl.NumberFormat(locale, {
+    style: "currency",
+    currency,
+    useGrouping: forceGrouping ? "always" : "auto",
+  }).format(Number(value));
 }

const locale = uiLocale(Astro.cookies, Astro.url);
const t = (text: string) => translate(locale, text);
const base = "/admin/o/" + organizationId;
const requestedPeriodId = Astro.url.searchParams.get("periodId");

let organization: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getOrganization>> | null = null;
let allPeriods: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listPeriods>> = [];
let summary: DashboardSummary | null = null;
let setupComplete = true;
let setupProgress: SetupProgress | null = null;
let loadError = false;

try {
  summary = await withRequestDb(async (db) => {
    const org = await getOrganization(db, organizationId);
    const periods = (await listPeriods(db, organizationId)).filter(
      (period) => !period.openingReadings
    );
    const { complete, progress } = await getSetupStatus(
      db,
      organizationId,
      org
    );

    // Keep the organization and setup shell available if only the summary fails.
    organization = org;
    allPeriods = periods;
    setupComplete = complete;
    setupProgress = progress;

    let periodId: string | undefined = requestedPeriodId || undefined;
    if (!periodId) {
      const openPeriod = await getCurrentOpenPeriod(db, organizationId);
      if (openPeriod && !openPeriod.openingReadings) {
        periodId = openPeriod.id;
      } else if (periods.length > 0) {
        periodId = periods[0].id;
      }
    }

    return periodId
      ? await getDashboardSummary(db, organizationId, periodId)
      : null;
  });
} catch (err) {
  if (err instanceof NotFoundError || err instanceof ForbiddenError) {
    return Astro.redirect(`${base}/periods`);
  }
  loadError = true;
}

const currentIndex = summary
  ? allPeriods.findIndex((period) => period.id === summary!.period.id)
  : -1;
const previousPeriod =
  currentIndex >= 0 && currentIndex < allPeriods.length - 1
    ? allPeriods[currentIndex + 1]
    : null;
const nextPeriod = currentIndex > 0 ? allPeriods[currentIndex - 1] : null;

const statuses = [
  "MISSING_DATA",
  "READY",
  "DRAFT",
  "PREPARED",
  "OVERDUE",
  "SENT",
  "PAID",
] as const;

const setupSteps = setupProgress
  ? [
      {
        title: t("Organization details"),
        done: setupProgress.steps.organization,
        href: `${base}/setup/organization`,
      },
      {
        title: t("Dwellings"),
        done: setupProgress.steps.dwellings,
        href: `${base}/setup/dwellings`,
      },
      {
        title: t("First billing period"),
        done: setupProgress.steps.period,
        href: `${base}/setup/period`,
      },
      {
        title: t("Charges"),
        done: setupProgress.steps.charges,
        href: `${base}/setup/charges`,
      },
      {
        title: t("Meters and starting readings"),
        done: setupProgress.steps.meters,
        href: `${base}/setup/meters`,
      },
    ]
  : [];
const completedSetupSteps = setupSteps.filter((step) => step.done).length;
const nextSetupStep = setupSteps.find((step) => !step.done);

const workbenchPath = summary
  ? `${base}/periods/${summary.period.id}`
  : `${base}/periods`;
const firstMissingItem = summary?.needsAttention.find(
  (item) => item.status === "MISSING_DATA"
);
const readingsUrl = firstMissingItem ? new URL(Astro.url) : null;
if (readingsUrl && firstMissingItem) {
  readingsUrl.searchParams.set("drawer", "readings");
  readingsUrl.searchParams.set("dwelling", firstMissingItem.dwellingId);
  readingsUrl.searchParams.delete("focus");
}

function nextActionSentence(value: DashboardSummary): string {
  const { key, count } = value.nextAction;
  if (key === "ENTER_READINGS")
    return plural(
      locale,
      count,
      "{n} dwelling still needs a reading.",
      "{n} dwellings still need readings."
    );
  if (key === "GENERATE_INVOICES")
    return plural(
      locale,
      count,
      "{n} invoice can be created.",
      "{n} invoices can be created."
    );
  if (key === "SEND_INVOICES")
    return plural(
      locale,
      count,
      "{n} invoice is ready to send.",
      "{n} invoices are ready to send."
    );
  if (key === "REVIEW_PAYMENTS")
    return plural(
      locale,
      count,
      "{n} invoice is waiting for payment.",
      "{n} invoices are waiting for payment."
    );
  return t(
    key === "CREATE_NEXT_PERIOD"
      ? "This month is closed. Create the next month to continue."
      : "Everything for this month is done."
  );
}

const actionLabel = summary
  ? {
      ENTER_READINGS: "Enter readings",
      GENERATE_INVOICES: "Generate invoices",
      SEND_INVOICES: "Send invoices",
      REVIEW_PAYMENTS: "Review payments",
      CREATE_NEXT_PERIOD: "Create next month",
      ALL_DONE: "Open workbench",
    }[summary.nextAction.key]
  : null;
const actionHref = summary
  ? {
      ENTER_READINGS: readingsUrl
        ? readingsUrl.pathname + readingsUrl.search
        : `${workbenchPath}?status=MISSING_DATA`,
      GENERATE_INVOICES: `${workbenchPath}?status=READY`,
      SEND_INVOICES: `${workbenchPath}?status=DRAFT`,
      REVIEW_PAYMENTS: `${base}/payments`,
      CREATE_NEXT_PERIOD: `${base}/periods#create-period`,
      ALL_DONE: workbenchPath,
    }[summary.nextAction.key]
  : null;

type ProgressState = "done" | "current" | "unavailable";
const currentProgressIndex = summary
  ? {
      ENTER_READINGS: 0,
      GENERATE_INVOICES: 1,
      SEND_INVOICES: 2,
      REVIEW_PAYMENTS: 3,
      CREATE_NEXT_PERIOD: -1,
      ALL_DONE: -1,
    }[summary.nextAction.key]
  : -1;
const progressSteps = summary
  ? [
      {
        label: t("Readings entered"),
        done: summary.progress.readingsDone,
        total: summary.progress.dwellingsTotal,
      },
      {
        label: t("Invoices created"),
        done: summary.progress.invoicesGenerated,
        total: summary.progress.dwellingsTotal,
      },
      {
        label: t("Invoices sent"),
        done: summary.progress.invoicesSent,
        total: summary.progress.dwellingsTotal,
      },
      {
        label: t("Payments received"),
        done: summary.progress.invoicesPaid,
        total: summary.progress.dwellingsTotal,
      },
    ].map((step, index) => ({
      ...step,
      state: (currentProgressIndex < 0
        ? "done"
        : index < currentProgressIndex
          ? "done"
          : index === currentProgressIndex
            ? "current"
            : "unavailable") as ProgressState,
    }))
  : [];
const progressStateText: Record<ProgressState, string> = {
  done: t("Done"),
  current: t("Now"),
  unavailable: t("Not available yet"),
};
const progressStateIcon: Record<ProgressState, IconName> = {
  done: "check",
  current: "clock",
  unavailable: "lock",
};

const deadlines = summary
  ? [
      summary.deadlines.readingDeadline
        ? {
            label: t("Readings due"),
            date: summary.deadlines.readingDeadline,
          }
        : null,
      {
        label: t("Invoices issued"),
        date: summary.deadlines.invoiceIssueDate,
      },
      {
        label: t("Payment due"),
        date: summary.deadlines.invoiceDueDate,
      },
    ].filter(
      (deadline): deadline is { label: string; date: string } => !!deadline
    )
  : [];

const manualAdjustment = summary
  ? subtractExact(
      summary.amountDue,
      subtractExact(
        sumExact([
          summary.currentCharges,
          summary.carriedOutstanding,
          summary.lateFees,
        ]),
        summary.creditsApplied
      )
    )
  : "0.00";
const hasManualAdjustment = Number(manualAdjustment) !== 0;

function formatMoney(
  value: string,
  currency: string,
  _displayLocale: typeof locale = locale
): string {
  return baseFormatMoney(value, currency, locale, true);
}

function formatDeduction(value: string, currency: string): string {
  const money = formatMoney(value, currency);
  return Number(value) === 0 ? money : `−${money}`;
}

type MissingItem =
  | { label: string | null; meterType: string; reason?: "NO_READING" }
  | { billingRuleId: string; ruleName: string; reason?: "NO_MANUAL_INPUT" }
  | {
      billingRuleId: string;
      ruleName: string;
      meterType: string;
      reason: "NO_ACTIVE_METER_FOR_RULE";
    }
  | { reason: "NO_APPLICABLE_RULES" }
  | string;

function missingReasons(items: unknown[]): string {
  return (items as MissingItem[])
    .map((item) => {
      if (typeof item === "string") {
        const legacyReading = item.match(
          /^Missing current-period meter reading for dwelling (.+?)\.?$/
        );
        return legacyReading
          ? fill(
              t("Missing current-period meter reading for dwelling {number}"),
              {
                number: legacyReading[1],
              }
            )
          : item;
      }
      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return "";
      if (item.reason === "NO_APPLICABLE_RULES")
        return t("No billing rule applies");
      if (item.reason === "NO_ACTIVE_METER_FOR_RULE")
        return `${item.ruleName} — ${t("no active meter")}`;
      if ("billingRuleId" in item) return item.ruleName;
      return item.label ?? entityLabel(item.meterType, locale);
    })
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ");
}

type AttentionRow =
  | { kind: "case"; item: DashboardSummary["needsAttention"][number] }
  | {
      kind: "summary";
      key: string;
      label: string;
      href: string;
      actionLabel: string;
      tone: "info" | "warning" | "danger";
      icon: IconName;
    };

const attentionRows: AttentionRow[] = summary
  ? summary.needsAttention.map((item) => ({ kind: "case" as const, item }))
  : [];
if (summary?.unmatchedPaymentCount) {
  attentionRows.push({
    kind: "summary",
    key: "unmatched",
    label: plural(
      locale,
      summary.unmatchedPaymentCount,
      "{n} bank payment is not matched to an invoice yet.",
      "{n} bank payments are not matched to an invoice yet."
    ),
    href: `${base}/payments?view=UNMATCHED`,
    actionLabel: "Review payments",
    tone: "warning",
    icon: "alert-triangle",
  });
}
if (summary?.proposedPaymentCount) {
  attentionRows.push({
    kind: "summary",
    key: "proposed",
    label: plural(
      locale,
      summary.proposedPaymentCount,
      "{n} suggested payment match is waiting for your check.",
      "{n} suggested payment matches are waiting for your check."
    ),
    href: `${base}/payments?view=PROPOSED`,
    actionLabel: "Review payments",
    tone: "info",
    icon: "clock",
  });
}
if (summary?.deliveryFailureCount) {
  attentionRows.push({
    kind: "summary",
    key: "delivery",
    label: plural(
      locale,
      summary.deliveryFailureCount,
      "{n} invoice could not be delivered.",
      "{n} invoices could not be delivered."
    ),
    href: `${workbenchPath}?status=PREPARED`,
    actionLabel: "Review failed deliveries",
    tone: "danger",
    icon: "alert-triangle",
  });
}
if (summary?.caseStatusCounts.READY) {
  attentionRows.push({
    kind: "summary",
    key: "ready",
    label: plural(
      locale,
      summary.caseStatusCounts.READY,
      "{n} invoice can be created.",
      "{n} invoices can be created."
    ),
    href: `${workbenchPath}?status=READY`,
    actionLabel: "Generate invoices",
    tone: "info",
    icon: "receipt-euro",
  });
}
const readyToSendCount = summary
  ? summary.caseStatusCounts.DRAFT + summary.caseStatusCounts.PREPARED
  : 0;
if (readyToSendCount) {
  attentionRows.push({
    kind: "summary",
    key: "send",
    label: plural(
      locale,
      readyToSendCount,
      "{n} invoice is ready to send.",
      "{n} invoices are ready to send."
    ),
    href: `${workbenchPath}?status=DRAFT`,
    actionLabel: "Send invoices",
    tone: "info",
    icon: "send",
  });
}
if (summary?.caseStatusCounts.SENT) {
  attentionRows.push({
    kind: "summary",
    key: "waiting",
    label: plural(
      locale,
      summary.caseStatusCounts.SENT,
      "{n} invoice is waiting for payment.",
      "{n} invoices are waiting for payment."
    ),
    href: `${base}/payments`,
    actionLabel: "Review payments",
    tone: "info",
    icon: "clock",
  });
}

const visibleAttentionRows = attentionRows.slice(0, 5);
const attentionItemCount = attentionRows.length;
const attentionAllHref = summary?.caseStatusCounts.MISSING_DATA
  ? `${workbenchPath}?status=MISSING_DATA`
  : summary?.caseStatusCounts.OVERDUE
    ? `${workbenchPath}?status=OVERDUE`
    : summary?.caseStatusCounts.READY
      ? `${workbenchPath}?status=READY`
      : readyToSendCount
        ? `${workbenchPath}?status=DRAFT`
        : `${base}/payments`;

function readingsHref(
  item: DashboardSummary["needsAttention"][number]
): string {
  const url = new URL(Astro.url);
  url.searchParams.set("drawer", "readings");
  url.searchParams.set("dwelling", item.dwellingId);
  url.searchParams.delete("focus");
  return url.pathname + url.search;
}

<AdminLayout
  title={t("Dashboard") + (organization ? " - " + organization.name : "")}
  organizationId={organizationId}
  organizationName={organization?.name}
>
  {
    loadError || !organization ? (
      <section class="dashboard-error panel" role="alert">
        <Icon name="alert-triangle" size="lg" />
        <div>
          <h1>{t("The overview could not be loaded.")}</h1>
          <p>{t("Try again in a moment, or open the billing periods.")}</p>
          <a class="btn-secondary" href={`${base}/periods`}>
            {t("Billing periods")}
          </a>
        </div>
      </section>
    ) : (
      <>
        {!setupComplete && setupProgress && (
          <section
            class="dashboard-setup panel"
            aria-labelledby="dashboard-setup-title"
          >
            <div class="setup-banner-intro">
              <div class="setup-banner-title">
                <h2 id="dashboard-setup-title">{t("Finish setting up")}</h2>
                <span class="setup-banner-count">
                  {fill(t("Setup steps done: {done} of {total}"), {
                    done: completedSetupSteps,
                    total: setupSteps.length,
                  })}
                </span>
              </div>
              {nextSetupStep && (
                <p class="setup-banner-next">
                  {t("Next step")}: <strong>{nextSetupStep.title}</strong>
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
            <ol class="setup-banner-steps" aria-label={t("Setup steps")}>
              {setupSteps.map((step, index) => (
                <li>
                  <a
                    href={step.href}
                    class:list={[
                      "setup-banner-step",
                      {
                        "is-done": step.done,
                        "is-next": step === nextSetupStep,
                      },
                    ]}
                  >
                    <span class="setup-banner-symbol" aria-hidden="true">
                      {step.done ? <Icon name="check" size="xs" /> : index + 1}
                    </span>
                    <span>
                      {step.title}
                      <span class="sr-only">
                        : {t(step.done ? "Done" : "To do")}
                      </span>
                    </span>
                  </a>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
            <a
              class="setup-banner-continue btn-primary"
              href={nextSetupStep?.href ?? `${base}/setup`}
            >
              {t("Continue setup")}
            </a>
          </section>
        )}

        {!summary ? (
          <div class="space-y-6">
            <header class="page-header">
              <div>
                <p class="eyebrow">{organization.name}</p>
                <h1>{t("Dashboard")}</h1>
                <p class="page-description">
                  {t("Monitor billing, payments and work requiring attention.")}
                </p>
              </div>
            </header>
            {setupComplete && (
              <EmptyState
                ornament
                title={t("No billing periods yet.")}
                description={t(
                  "Create a period to start the monthly billing workflow."
                )}
              >
                <a class="btn-primary" href={`${base}/periods#create-period`}>
                  {t("Create period")}
                </a>
              </EmptyState>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div class="dashboard-page">
            <AdminContextDrawer
              organizationId={organizationId}
              periodId={summary.period.id}
              periodStatus={summary.period.status}
              periodName={formatPeriod(
                summary.period.year,
                summary.period.month,
                locale
              )}
              workbenchPath={workbenchPath}
              refreshSelectors={[
                ".dashboard-month-status",
                ".dashboard-money",
                ".dashboard-attention",
                ".dashboard-more",
              ]}
            />

            <header class="dashboard-header">
              <div>
                <p class="eyebrow">{organization.name}</p>
                <div class="dashboard-title-row">
                  <h1 class="font-display">

                      summary.period.year,
                      summary.period.month,
                      locale
                    )}
                  </h1>
                  <StatusBadge status={summary.period.status} />

                <p
                  class="dashboard-period-context"

                    summary.period.status !== "LOCKED" && summary.isNewestPeriod
                      ? "true"
                      : undefined

                  !summary.isNewestPeriod ? (
                    <>
                      <Icon
                        name={
                          summary.period.status === "LOCKED" ? "lock" : "clock"
                        }
                        size="xs"
                      />
                      {t(
                        !summary.isNewestPeriod
                          ? "You are viewing an earlier month."
                          : "This month is closed."

                      firstMissingItem
                        ? "readings"
                        : undefined

                        : undefined

                        : undefined
                    }

              <div class="month-status-meta">

                  <strong>{t("Coming up")}</strong>
                  {deadlines.map((deadline) => (
                    <span>
                      {deadline.label}: {formatDate(deadline.date, locale)}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                </p>
                {setupComplete && (
                  <div
                    class="dashboard-readiness"
                    aria-label={t("Billing readiness")}
                  >
                    <strong>{t("Billing readiness")}</strong>
                    <ul>
                      <li data-ready="true">
                        <Icon name="check" size="xs" />
                        {t("Setup complete")}
                      </li>
                      <li data-ready={String(summary.readiness.billingReady)}>
                        <Icon
                          name={
                            summary.readiness.billingReady ? "check" : "clock"
                          }
                          size="xs"
                        />
                        {t("All readings entered")}:{" "}
                        {t(summary.readiness.billingReady ? "Done" : "To do")}
                      </li>
                      <li data-ready={String(summary.readiness.readyToSend)}>
                        <Icon
                          name={
                            summary.readiness.readyToSend ? "check" : "clock"
                          }
                          size="xs"
                        />
                        {t("Ready to send")}:{" "}
                        {t(summary.readiness.readyToSend ? "Done" : "To do")}
                      </li>
                    </ul>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </section>

            <section class="dashboard-money" aria-labelledby="money-title">
              <div class="dashboard-section-heading">
                <div>
                  <h2 id="money-title">{t("Money this month")}</h2>
                  <p>{t("Includes invoices that are not sent yet.")}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="dashboard-money-kpis">
                <KpiCard
                  icon="invoice"
                  label={t("Total to pay")}
                  value={formatMoney(
                    summary.amountDue,
                    organization.currency,
                    locale
                  )}
                />
                <KpiCard
                  icon="coins"
                  label={t("Received in payments")}
                  tone="success"
                  value={formatMoney(
                    summary.totalPaid,
                    organization.currency,
                    locale
                  )}
                />
                <KpiCard
                  icon="clock"
                  label={t("Still unpaid")}
                  tone={
                    summary.caseStatusCounts.OVERDUE > 0 ? "danger" : "neutral"
                  }
                  value={formatMoney(
                    summary.totalOutstanding,
                    organization.currency,
                    locale
                  )}
                />
              </div>
              <div class="money-reconciliation panel">
                <dl aria-label={t("Money this month")}>
                  <div>
                    <dt>{t("This month's invoices")}</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {formatMoney(
                        summary.currentCharges,
                        organization.currency,
                        locale
                      )}
                    </dd>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <dt>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">+</span> {t("Previous debt")}
                    </dt>
                    <dd>
                      {formatMoney(
                        summary.carriedOutstanding,
                        organization.currency,
                        locale
                      )}
                    </dd>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <dt>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">−</span>{" "}
                      {t("Previous overpayment")}
                    </dt>
                    <dd>
                      {formatDeduction(
                        summary.creditsApplied,
                        organization.currency
                      )}
                    </dd>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <dt>
                      <span aria-hidden="true">+</span> {t("Late fees")}
                    </dt>
                    <dd>
                      {formatMoney(
                        summary.lateFees,
                        organization.currency,
                        locale
                      )}
                    </dd>
                  </div>
                  {hasManualAdjustment && (
                    <div>
                      <dt>{t("Manual adjustment")}</dt>
                      <dd>
                        {formatMoney(
                          manualAdjustment,
                          organization.currency,
                          locale
                        )}
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div class="money-total">
                    <dt>{t("Total to pay")}</dt>
                    <dd>
                      {formatMoney(
                        summary.amountDue,
                        organization.currency,
                        locale
                      )}
                    </dd>
                  </div>
                </dl>
                {!hasManualAdjustment && (
                  <p>
                    {t(
                      "Total due = this month's invoices + previous debt − previous overpayment + late fees."
                    )}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
            </section>

            <section
              class="dashboard-attention"
              aria-labelledby="dashboard-attention-title"
            >
              <div class="dashboard-section-heading">
                <div>
                  <h2 id="dashboard-attention-title">{t("Needs attention")}</h2>
                  <p>{attentionItemCount}</p>
                </div>
                {attentionRows.length > 5 && (
                  <a href={attentionAllHref}>
                    {fill(t("Show all ({n})"), { n: attentionItemCount })}
                    <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                  </a>
                )}
              </div>

              {visibleAttentionRows.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState title={t("Nothing needs attention right now.")} />
              ) : (
                <div
                  class="attention-list panel"
                  role="list"
                  aria-label={t("Needs attention")}
                >
                  {visibleAttentionRows.map((row) =>
                    row.kind === "case" ? (
                      <article class="attention-row" role="listitem">
                        <div class="attention-row-main">
                          <div class="attention-heading">
                            <a
                              href={`${base}/dwellings/${row.item.dwellingId}`}
                            >
                              {t("Dwelling")} {row.item.dwellingNumber}
                            </a>
                            <StatusBadge status={row.item.status} />
                          </div>
                          {row.item.status === "OVERDUE" ? (
                            <>
                              <p>
                                {t(
                                  "Payment is past its due date. Review reconciliation."
                                )}
                              </p>
                              <p class="attention-details">
                                <strong>
                                  {formatMoney(
                                    row.item.overdueAmount ?? "0.00",
                                    organization.currency,
                                    locale
                                  )}
                                </strong>
                                {row.item.dueDate && (
                                  <span>
                                    {fill(t("Due {date}"), {
                                      date: formatDate(
                                        row.item.dueDate,
                                        locale
                                      ),
                                    })}
                                  </span>
                                )}
                                {row.item.daysLate !== null && (
                                  <span>
                                    {plural(
                                      locale,
                                      row.item.daysLate,
                                      "{n} day late",
                                      "{n} days late"
                                    )}
                                  </span>
                                )}
                              </p>
                            </>
                          ) : (
                            <p>
                              {missingReasons(row.item.missingData) ||
                                t(
                                  "Readings are required before generating an invoice."
                                )}
                            </p>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        {row.item.status === "MISSING_DATA" &&
                        summary.period.status !== "LOCKED" ? (
                          <a
                            class="attention-action"
                            href={readingsHref(row.item)}
                            data-open-drawer="readings"
                            data-dwelling-id={row.item.dwellingId}
                            aria-haspopup="dialog"
                            aria-controls="admin-context-drawer"
                          >
                            {t("Enter readings")}
                            <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                          </a>
                        ) : (
                          <a class="attention-action" href={`${base}/payments`}>
                            {t("Review payments")}
                            <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                          </a>
                        )}
                      </article>
                    ) : (
                      <article
                        class="attention-row attention-summary-row"
                        data-tone={row.tone}
                        role="listitem"
                      >
                        <span class="attention-summary-icon" aria-hidden="true">
                          <Icon name={row.icon} size="sm" />
                        </span>
                        <p>{row.label}</p>
                        <a class="attention-action" href={row.href}>
                          {t(row.actionLabel)}
                          <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                        </a>
                      </article>
                    )
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </section>

            <details class="dashboard-more panel">
              <summary>{t("Additional information")}</summary>
              <div class="dashboard-more-grid">
                <section aria-labelledby="case-status-title">
                  <h2 id="case-status-title">{t("Case status")}</h2>
                  <div class="dashboard-status-list">
                    {statuses.map((status) => (
                      <a href={`${workbenchPath}?status=${status}`}>
                        <StatusBadge status={status} />
                        <span>
                          {summary.caseStatusCounts[status]}
                          <Icon name="arrow-right" size="xs" />
                        </span>
                      </a>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </section>
                <section aria-labelledby="consumption-title">
                  <h2 id="consumption-title">
                    <Icon name="droplet" size="sm" />
                    {t("Consumption")}
                  </h2>
                  <dl class="consumption-list">
                    <div>
                      <dt>{t("Cold water")}</dt>
                      <dd>
                        {formatNumber(summary.coldWaterConsumption, locale)} m³
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <dt>{t("Hot water")}</dt>
                      <dd>
                        {formatNumber(summary.hotWaterConsumption, locale)} m³
                      </dd>
                    </div>
                  </dl>
                  {summary.carriedForwardReadingCount > 0 && (
                    <p class="carried-readings-note">
                      {plural(
                        locale,
                        summary.carriedForwardReadingCount,
                        "Includes {n} reading carried over from the previous month.",
                        "Includes {n} readings carried over from the previous month."
                      )}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </section>
              </div>
            </details>
          </div>
        )}
      </>
    )
  }
</AdminLayout>

+type MissingItem =
+  | { label: string | null; meterType: string; reason?: "NO_READING" }
+  | { billingRuleId: string; ruleName: string; reason?: "NO_MANUAL_INPUT" }
+  | {

+function missingReasons(items: unknown[]): string {
+  return (items as MissingItem[])

+          ? fill(
+              t("Missing current-period meter reading for dwelling {number}"),
+              {

+              }

+      }
+      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return "";
+      if (item.reason === "NO_APPLICABLE_RULES")
+        return t("No billing rule applies");
+      if (item.reason === "NO_ACTIVE_METER_FOR_RULE")
+        return `${item.ruleName} — ${t("no active meter")}`;
+      if ("billingRuleId" in item) return item.ruleName;

+    })

+    .join(", ");

+  current: "clock",
+  unavailable: "lock",
+};

+  ? [
+      summary.deadlines.readingDeadline
+        ? {
+            label: t("Readings due"),
+            date: summary.deadlines.readingDeadline,

+      (deadline): deadline is { label: string; date: string } => !!deadline

+  ? subtractExact(
+      summary.amountDue,
+      subtractExact(
+        sumExact([
+          summary.currentCharges,
+          summary.carriedOutstanding,
+          summary.lateFees,
+        ]),
+        summary.creditsApplied

+    )
+  : "0.00";
+const hasManualAdjustment = Number(manualAdjustment) !== 0;

+function formatMoney(
+  value: string,
+  currency: string,
+  _displayLocale: typeof locale = locale
+): string {
+  return baseFormatMoney(value, currency, locale, true);
+}
+
+function formatDeduction(value: string, currency: string): string {
+  const money = formatMoney(value, currency);
+  return Number(value) === 0 ? money : `−${money}`;
+}
+
+type MissingItem =
+  | { label: string | null; meterType: string; reason?: "NO_READING" }
+  | { billingRuleId: string; ruleName: string; reason?: "NO_MANUAL_INPUT" }
+  | {
+      billingRuleId: string;
+      ruleName: string;
+      meterType: string;
+      reason: "NO_ACTIVE_METER_FOR_RULE";
+    }
+  | { reason: "NO_APPLICABLE_RULES" }
+  | string;
+
+function missingReasons(items: unknown[]): string {
+  return (items as MissingItem[])
+    .map((item) => {
+      if (typeof item === "string") {
+        const legacyReading = item.match(
+          /^Missing current-period meter reading for dwelling (.+?)\.?$/
+        );
+        return legacyReading
+          ? fill(
+              t("Missing current-period meter reading for dwelling {number}"),
+              {
+                number: legacyReading[1],
+              }
+            )
+          : item;
+      }
+      if (!item || typeof item !== "object") return "";
+      if (item.reason === "NO_APPLICABLE_RULES")
+        return t("No billing rule applies");
+      if (item.reason === "NO_ACTIVE_METER_FOR_RULE")
+        return `${item.ruleName} — ${t("no active meter")}`;
+      if ("billingRuleId" in item) return item.ruleName;
+      return item.label ?? entityLabel(item.meterType, locale);
+    })
+    .filter(Boolean)
+    .join(", ");
+}
+
+type AttentionRow =
+  | { kind: "case"; item: DashboardSummary["needsAttention"][number] }
+  | {
+      kind: "summary";
+      key: string;
+      label: string;
+      href: string;
+      actionLabel: string;
+      tone: "info" | "warning" | "danger";
+      icon: IconName;
+    };
+
+const attentionRows: AttentionRow[] = summary
+  ? summary.needsAttention.map((item) => ({ kind: "case" as const, item }))
+  : [];
+if (summary?.unmatchedPaymentCount) {
+  attentionRows.push({
+    kind: "summary",
+    key: "unmatched",
+    label: plural(
+      locale,
+      summary.unmatchedPaymentCount,
+      "{n} bank payment is not matched to an invoice yet.",
+      "{n} bank payments are not matched to an invoice yet."
+    ),
+    href: `${base}/payments?view=UNMATCHED`,
+    actionLabel: "Review payments",
+    tone: "warning",
+    icon: "alert-triangle",
+  });
+}
+if (summary?.proposedPaymentCount) {
+  attentionRows.push({
+    kind: "summary",
+    key: "proposed",
+    label: plural(
+      locale,
+      summary.proposedPaymentCount,
+      "{n} suggested payment match is waiting for your check.",
+      "{n} suggested payment matches are waiting for your check."
+    ),
+    href: `${base}/payments?view=PROPOSED`,
+    actionLabel: "Review payments",
+    tone: "info",
+    icon: "clock",
+  });
+}
+if (summary?.deliveryFailureCount) {
+  attentionRows.push({
+    kind: "summary",
+    key: "delivery",
+    label: plural(
+      locale,
+      summary.deliveryFailureCount,
+      "{n} invoice could not be delivered.",
+      "{n} invoices could not be delivered."
+    ),
+    href: `${workbenchPath}?status=PREPARED`,
+    actionLabel: "Review failed deliveries",
+    tone: "danger",
+    icon: "alert-triangle",
+  });
+}
+if (summary?.caseStatusCounts.READY) {
+  attentionRows.push({
+    kind: "summary",
+    key: "ready",
+    label: plural(
+      locale,
+      summary.caseStatusCounts.READY,
+      "{n} invoice can be created.",
+      "{n} invoices can be created."
+    ),
+    href: `${workbenchPath}?status=READY`,
+    actionLabel: "Generate invoices",
+    tone: "info",
+    icon: "receipt-euro",
+  });
+}
+const readyToSendCount = summary
+  ? summary.caseStatusCounts.DRAFT + summary.caseStatusCounts.PREPARED
+  : 0;
+if (readyToSendCount) {
+  attentionRows.push({
+    kind: "summary",
+    key: "send",
+    label: plural(
+      locale,
+      readyToSendCount,
+      "{n} invoice is ready to send.",
+      "{n} invoices are ready to send."
+    ),
+    href: `${workbenchPath}?status=DRAFT`,
+    actionLabel: "Send invoices",
+    tone: "info",
+    icon: "send",
+  });
+}
+if (summary?.caseStatusCounts.SENT) {
+  attentionRows.push({
+    kind: "summary",
+    key: "waiting",
+    label: plural(
+      locale,
+      summary.caseStatusCounts.SENT,
+      "{n} invoice is waiting for payment.",
+      "{n} invoices are waiting for payment."
+    ),
+    href: `${base}/payments`,
+    actionLabel: "Review payments",
+    tone: "info",
+    icon: "clock",
+  });
+}
+
+function attentionPriority(row: AttentionRow): number {
+  if (row.kind === "case") return row.item.status === "OVERDUE" ? 2 : 3;
+  if (row.key === "delivery") return 0;
+  if (row.key === "unmatched") return 1;
+  return 4;
+}
+
+attentionRows.sort((a, b) => attentionPriority(a) - attentionPriority(b));
+
+const showAllAttention = Astro.url.searchParams.get("all") === "1";
+const visibleAttentionRows = showAllAttention
+  ? attentionRows
+  : attentionRows.slice(0, 5);
+const attentionItemCount = attentionRows.length;
+const attentionAllUrl = new URL(Astro.url);
+attentionAllUrl.searchParams.set("all", "1");
+const attentionAllHref = attentionAllUrl.pathname + attentionAllUrl.search;
+
+function readingsHref(
+  item: DashboardSummary["needsAttention"][number]
+): string {
+  const url = new URL(Astro.url);
+  url.searchParams.set("drawer", "readings");
+  url.searchParams.set("dwelling", item.dwellingId);
+  url.searchParams.delete("focus");
+  return url.pathname + url.search;
+}
